(4) **Solicitor side — the 4 compliance artefacts `app/CLAUDE.md`'s own BUILD PLAN flagged as missing, now built:** conflict-check + client-ID/verification records (new step 3 in the `NewConsultation.tsx` intake wizard — plain factual records, no LLM, no draft banner needed) and costs disclosure (LPUL s174/s178) + costs agreement (s180) generation (new "Compliance" tab in `SessionReview.tsx` — solicitor supplies the numbers/scope, Claude drafts the surrounding structure, tier auto-computed from estimate, hard **DRAFT — not lawyer-reviewed, do not send to client** banner, and the 4 statutory rights statements are left as a `[LAWYER TO CONFIRM EXACT WORDING]` placeholder rather than fabricated — deliberately not guessing at prescribed text). Also force-captured the 2 negligence-critical file-note fields from *Sewell v Zelden* (risks warned of / client's response) into the case-brief schema. **🔴 Found + fixed a systemic pre-existing bug while verifying the intake wizard:** all 19 "create" functions in `server/db.ts` read `(result as any).insertId`, but the promise-based mysql2 pool (switched to earlier to fix a different stall) returns a `[ResultSetHeader, FieldPacket[]]` tuple, not a plain object — so `.insertId` was `undefined` everywhere, silently. DB rows were always written correctly; only the *returned* object was broken, which broke any flow needing the new row's ID immediately (e.g. client→session chaining in the intake wizard) while insert-and-later-list flows looked fine. Fixed all 19 call sites, verified with a direct DB round-trip. Also fixed along the way: AustLII fallback mislabeling hallucinated citations as `"austlii_live"` (same green badge as a real scrape hit — a real professional-liability bug), and `invokeAnthropic()` silently dropping `response_format` (any JSON-schema call to Claude could get markdown-fenced text back and crash `JSON.parse()`). **Verified live, non-mocked, for every feature above** — real DB round-trips, real Claude calls across all 3 costs-disclosure threshold bands ($500→none, $1,500→standard, $5,000→full), test data cleaned up after each pass. **Not done / explicitly deferred, not silently dropped:** 3b Deepgram transcription (Peter's own account-creation action — nothing downstream can be end-to-end tested until this lands), lawyer review of the 2 new templates (the actual gate for real client use — the build was never the blocker), PI insurance, AU data residency, ToS/privacy lawyer pass. **A friend's urgent parenting-orders situation (US-citizen ex, AU-citizen client, baby in Sydney) came up mid-session — Peter deferred giving her access until the app is closer to ready; revisit before offering access, not a build task.**)

**Last updated:** 2026-07-14 (later) · Claude Code (🟢 **APP IS LIVE IN-BROWSER at `localhost:3000/metis` — all-Anthropic, Manus-free, and off iCloud.** Three fixes landed this session, in order: (1) **All-Anthropic stack** — Peter supplied an Anthropic key; `.env` now runs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` + `LLM_MODEL=claude-sonnet-5` for every AI call, Forge/Gemini vars commented out (rollback-safe), old `.env` backed up to `.env.backup-manus-era`. (2) **Manus OAuth SDK removed from the boot path** — `sdk.ts`'s `createOAuthHttpClient()` was stalling ~27s at import time (network attempt against an unset `OAUTH_SERVER_URL`); made lazy so it's never constructed under DEV_AUTH/magic-link auth. `oauth.ts` routes dropped from `index.ts` entirely. (3) **🔴 ROOT-CAUSE FIX — project moved off iCloud-synced Desktop.** `~/Desktop/metis` (and everyone's projects on Desktop generally) is under macOS's iCloud "Desktop & Documents" sync; `node_modules`-scale file counts caused iCloud to intermittently evict/lock files mid-read, producing the multi-minute-to-hours-long hangs seen throughout this session (esbuild `Cannot read file ... operation timed out`, a `pnpm install` that took forever via rsync copy, etc.). **Fix:** project content copied (rsync, verified byte-identical via `diff -rq`, `node_modules`/`dist`/`out` excluded and reinstalled fresh — 3.7s vs. many minutes) to **`~/dev/metis`** (outside iCloud's sync scope); old iCloud copy preserved as `~/Desktop/metis-OLD-iCloud-backup-2026-07-14` (not deleted — safe to remove once confirmed); **`~/Desktop/metis` is now a symlink to `~/dev/metis`**, so every existing doc/path reference still resolves. Cold homepage load went from **10–25s (or an outright hang) to 27ms**; `/metis` page 182ms; a live `metis.chat` round-trip verified correct + rails-clean (`gated:false`) from the new location. **⚠️ Same iCloud risk likely affects dsk/exit-code/eonia/hydralab** if any of those run node_modules-heavy tooling from Desktop — flagging for those STATUS files, not fixing here. **Also shipped this session (earlier):** `metis` router + `MetisChat.tsx` page wired into the solicitor app (`/metis` route, `AIChatBox`, TS-side UPL rails mirroring the Python `rails.py`), DB layer switched from `mysql2` callback pool to `mysql2/promise` (the callback pool + drizzle stalled indefinitely against local MySQL 9.6 — separate bug from the iCloud one, also fixed), local MySQL schema pushed (16 tables), DEV_AUTH made DB-independent (3s race fallback) so sign-in never blocks on a slow query. **Known open item:** drizzle's first query still occasionally stalls against local MySQL — worked around for DEV_AUTH, not yet root-caused; solicitor-side DB features (client records/sessions/portal) need this resolved before real testing.)

## State (3-line summary)

🟢 **TWO-SIDED AI FAMILY-LAW PRODUCT (NSW beachhead).** Metis Cortex is two products on one engine:

- **Metis Cortex (solicitor side)** — records the client conference → AI issue detection → live AustLII search → drafts file note + case brief + client proposal → client portal. **CODE-COMPLETE** at `~/Desktop/metis-cortex-app/` (React 19 + tRPC + Express + Drizzle/MySQL · magic-link auth · off Manus · 27 tests). **NOT deployed.** Client portal is currently **receive-only** (solicitor→client docs).
- **"Metis" (client side) — THE SOUL.** Conversational co-pilot that helps the *client* gather their own documents (user-supervised browser co-pilot for myGov/ATO/CSAOnline/courts + OCR). **Primary firm-facing flow (per Peter 2026-06-18): POST-first-meeting** — after the solicitor gives the client the document list, Metis walks them through actually getting each item and returning it, so the firm stops chasing paper for weeks. (The spec also covers a self-rep flow: building a court-ready 12-stage **Matter Pack** pre-engagement — Peter's own DPO matter is the prototype fixture.) Born from Peter's own family-law experience. **SPEC'D + 5 CLI prototypes only — NOT built.** Phase 1 sprint (2–19 Jun) did not ship it.

**The client-assist IS the solicitor value prop:** a prepared client (docs gathered fast + properly) = the solicitor's chasing + disorganised-consult pain solved. One offer, not two.

**Validation NOT started.** 7-firm Mom-Test list staged but never sent (`interview-tracker.csv` response columns all empty). Only family-law-solicitor contact is Peter's own lawyer **John Theodoropoulos @ Konstan Lawyers** (2 Metis emails 27 May + 11 Jun + a voicemail — no reply). Escalating to firm owner **Simon Konstantinidis** as **design partner + compliance reviewer** (NOT a free-trial pitch — the client tool isn't built).

**Offer/pricing/positioning all DECIDED but untested:** founding tiers **A$700 / A$1,800 / A$3,500 locked-for-life** (cap 5 firms); wedge = **AustLII-live + post-2024/2025 family-law cognition + NSW all-party-consent workflow**; sits on top of LEAP/Smokeball (no migration). **Killed:** auto costs-disclosure (s178 liability), the A$48,680 value stack, meetiris.au B2C sub-brand, trading-signals pivot.

**Gates before ANY third-party launch:** Phase 0 legaltech-lawyer UPL/ToS sign-off (browser co-pilot) · family-law compliance + template review (costs disclosure s174/s178 · *Sewell v Zelden* file note · engagement letter) · Lawcover PI · AU data residency · ToS/privacy lawyer pass.

Evaluated + rejected on three structural grounds: (1) **AFSL exposure existential** — algorithmic trading signals = textbook ASIC-regulated activity. Spinning up under same trust as EXIT CODE creates contagion risk that collapses EXIT CODE's "not-financial-advice" defense (Sophie Grace currently working that scope). Acquiring AFSL = 12-18 months + $50K NTA + responsible manager + compliance officer. (2) **Operator labor problem** — algo products require ongoing R&D + market-hours monitoring + edge maintenance + drawdown support. Directly violates the Hormozi principle we acted on TODAY when stripping the cohort from EXIT CODE Pro Bundle. (3) **Brand wedge erosion** — EXIT CODE's wedge is "no signals, no founder face, no profit claims, no track-record theatre." Sister product breaking all four undermines EXIT CODE positioning even with separate brand (sophisticated traders connect dots on Reddit within months). Decision: Metis Cortex stays in v3 lane (AI agency for service businesses — AI Audit + Trades AI Receptionist Grand Slam etc.). Quantiva-as-potential-B2B-client noted instead: algo trading companies need fast lead conversion → Metis Cortex's actual service offering applies. Could be a vertical Metis serves later, not become. Full analysis in EXIT CODE chat 2026-05-25; not re-pasted here to avoid duplication. |
